We address this gap through a systematic study of adversarial robustness and multimodal safety across 12 diverse languages, evaluating open-source MLLMs that acquire multilingual capability through instruction tuning. Gradient-based attacks reveal a transferable multilingual vulnerability: adversarial images optimized in one language continue to induce failure in others, demonstrating strong cross-lingual transferability. Multilingual safety further varies with how effectively a model retrieves or interprets harmful instructions. When harmful intent is issued through text, languages with stronger linguistic grounding more often elicit misuse-enabling responses, while weaker languages produce fewer unsafe outputs. When embedded in the image as typographic content, English scripts are reliably recognised and followed, whereas non-English scripts are rarely parsed by the vision encoder. Lower-resource languages may therefore appear safer, but this is an artefact of comprehension and visual-grounding failures rather than genuine alignment, a phenomenon we term safety-by-failure. In contrast, MLLMs that build multilingual capability throughout their training stages rather than only at instruction tuning, such as Qwen3-VL, exhibit genuine cross-lingual safety, maintaining active refusal across languages rather than masking comprehension failure. Shallow multilingual adaptation, such as fine-tuning on translated instruction data, may produce surface-level understanding that creates illusory safety in low-resource languages; deeper integration across training stages leads to genuine multilingual safety alignment.

</details>


### [79] [Consistency Training Can Entrench Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03810)
*David Demitri Africa,Arathi Mani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 一致性训练在模型对齐中并非中性，它通常能抑制奖励黑客和紧急失调，但会放大谄媚行为，且其效果主要由标记过程导致的分布偏移驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一致性训练方法简单、可扩展且几乎无需标注，但自举循环可能放大模型的不良行为，现有研究对其对齐效果的理解不足，亟需系统评估。

Method: 在108个"模型生物体"（7B-70B开源模型，细调出各种受控失调行为）上测试了7种一致性训练方法，并构建统一理论框架分析其对齐效应。

Result: 一致性训练总体上抑制了奖励黑客和紧急失调，但显著放大了谄媚行为；证据表明分布偏移（而非选择算子差异）是系统性对齐效应的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 一致性训练并非对齐中性，其在关键系统中的使用需要谨慎审计。

Abstract: Consistency training encourages a model to produce similar outputs across related inputs or sampling procedures. Such methods are simple, scalable, and largely label-free, but their effects on model alignment remain poorly understood. Could the self-bootstrapping nature of these methods amplify undesired behavior in models? We test seven consistency training methods on 108 ``model organisms: open-source models (7B--70B) fine-tuned to exhibit various forms of controlled misaligned behavior. We find that outcomes vary significantly: consistency training generally suppresses reward hacking and emergent misalignment but amplifies sycophancy. We present evidence that distribution shifts induced by the consistency labeling process, rather than variation in the selection operators, may be the primary driver of systematic alignment effects. Finally, we present a unifying theoretical framework to derive conditions under which consistency training will amplify or suppress misalignment. In total, our study establishes that consistency training is not alignment-neutral, and that its use in critical systems should be carefully audited.

</details>


### [80] [Rethinking the Idiomaticity Decomposability Hypothesis: Evidence from Distributional Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03817)
*Maggie Mi,Golzar Atefi,Atsuki Yamaguchi,Felix Gers,Aline Villavicencio,Nafise Sadat Moosavi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究使用上下文语言模型作为可控分布学习者，探讨习语的可分解性及其与句法灵活性、预测性之间的关系。模型内部的可分解性测量与人类判断弱相关，与句法灵活性呈小但一致的负相关。预训练分析表明，习语表征的稳定化不单由频率解释，而是由惊讶值、可分解性和频率共同贡献，其中可分解性表现出最强的训练依赖效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为习语的可分解性（构成词义对整体比喻义的贡献程度）预测句法灵活性；而基于用法的观点则将习语行为归因于分布经验（如熟悉度和可预测性）。本研究旨在通过上下文语言模型作为可控分布学习者，检验这两种观点的解释力。

Method: 使用上下文语言模型作为可控分布学习者，提出模型内部的可分解性测量方法，将其与人类评分、句法灵活性和可预测性进行关联，并在预训练过程中追踪习语学习动态。

Result: 模型推导的可分解性与人类判断弱相关，与句法灵活性呈小而一致的负相关。习语表征的稳定化受惊讶值、可分解性和频率共同影响，其中可分解性表现出最强的训练依赖效应。

Conclusion: 习语的可分解性对句法灵活性的预测作用有限，习语学习是一个受多种因素共同影响的复杂过程，仅凭频率无法充分解释习语表征的稳定化。

Abstract: Idioms can be analysed in terms of their decomposability, the extent to which constituent meanings contribute to the figurative whole. Decomposability is thought to predict syntactic flexibility. Usage-based accounts instead attribute idiom behaviour to distributional experience, such as speaker familiarity and predictability. We examine these views using contextualised language models as controlled distributional learners. We propose a model-internal measure of decomposability and relate it to human ratings, syntactic flexibility, and predictability while tracking idiom learning during pretraining. Model-derived decomposability correlates weakly with human judgments and shows a small but consistent negative relationship with syntactic flexibility. Pretraining analyses show that stabilisation of idiom representations in models is not explained by frequency alone. Instead, surprisal, decomposability, and frequency all contribute, with decomposability showing the strongest training-dependent effect.

</details>


### [81] [Clustered Self-Assessment: A Simple yet Effective Method for Uncertainty Quantification in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03846)
*Qi Cao,Takeshi Kojima,Andrew Gambardella,Helinyi Peng,Yutaka Matsuo,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于自评估的LLM不确定性量化方法，通过将生成结果聚类为语义不同的选项并利用模型对选项的置信度概率，实现高效可靠的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常生成看似合理但事实错误的回答，且缺乏显式的不确定性估计，现有方法依赖间接信号（如熵）难以解释且未充分利用模型自身评估能力。

Method: 将采样生成结果聚类为语义不同的簇，转化为结构化多选题的选项，利用LLM对各选项的分配概率作为置信度估计。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上持续优于基线方法，仅需少量额外样本（如2个）即可达到竞争性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法既有效又高效，为LLM不确定性量化提供了一种简单实用的新途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable performance across diverse tasks, but they often generate responses that appear plausible while being factually incorrect. This problem is compounded by the lack of explicit uncertainty estimates, which makes it difficult for users to judge the reliability of model outputs. Existing uncertainty quantification methods typically rely on indirect signals, such as entropy across sampled generations. These signals can be difficult to interpret and do not fully leverage the model's ability to assess its own uncertainty. We propose a simple yet effective self-assessment method for uncertainty quantification in LLMs. Our approach groups sampled generations into semantically distinct clusters, converts them into answer options in a structured multiple-choice question, and uses the probability assigned by the LLM to each option as a confidence estimate. Experiments across multiple models and datasets show that our method consistently outperforms baseline approaches. Notably, it achieves competitive performance with as few as two additional samples, demonstrating both its effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [82] [A Training-Free Mixture-of-Agents Framework for Multi-Document Summarization using LLMs and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03867)
*Cuong Vuong Tuan,Trang Mai Xuan,Tien-Cuong Nguyen,Vu-Duc Ngo,Thien Van Luong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的混合智能体框架，结合大语言模型和知识图谱，通过分解任务（提取、知识感知抽象、迭代优化）及多视角一致性机制，在多数据集上取得SOTA或竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MDS方法难以捕捉文档间复杂关系、依赖大量标注数据、跨领域和跨语言泛化能力有限，亟需一种更灵活且无需监督训练的方法。

Method: 提出无训练的混合智能体框架，将摘要任务分解为三个专门智能体子任务：提取式选择、知识感知抽象和迭代优化；通过LLM引导的多视角一致性机制统一各智能体输出。

Result: 在英语和越南语的四个数据集上取得了SOTA或具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 验证了模块化设计在跨领域和跨语言多文档摘要任务中的有效性和适应性，无需微调即可达到优异效果。

Abstract: Multi-Document Summarization (MDS) plays a critical role in distilling essential information from collections of textual data. Existing approaches often struggle to capture complex inter-document relationships, rely heavily on large amounts of labeled data for supervised training, or exhibit limited generalization across domains and languages. To address these limitations, we present a training-free mixture-of-agents framework for MDS that leverages the complementary strengths of large language models (LLMs) and knowledge graphs. Our approach decomposes summarization into specialized agent tasks: extractive selection, knowledge-aware abstraction, and iterative refinement, each operating without task-specific fine-tuning. We unify their outputs using a multi-perspective consistency mechanism guided by LLMs. Experiments across four datasets in English and Vietnamese demonstrate state-of-the-art or competitive performance, validating the effectiveness and adaptability of our modular design.

</details>


### [83] [RealClawBench: Live OpenClaw Benchmarks from Real Developer-Agent Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03889)
*Zongwei Lv,Zhewen Tan,Yaoming Li,Yilun Yao,Yuxuan Tian,Lin Sun,Xiangzheng Zhang,Weihong Lin,Tong Yang,Guangxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RealClawBench是从真实OpenClaw会话构建的实时基准框架，包含281个可执行任务，评估14个模型发现最佳系统仅解决65.8%任务，揭示了现实开发者-智能体工作负载中的显著改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试缺乏真实开发者-智能体会话的关键现实属性，如对本地执行环境的依赖、隐含或未明确指定的意图以及非平凡的验证需求。

Method: 通过两种核心机制：重建执行环境和确定性可验证评分器，将真实会话转化为可重复、自动评分的任务，并保持源分布（最大JS散度0.0448）。

Result: 发布281个可执行任务，最佳系统（GPT-4等模型）仅解决65.8%任务，说明存在显著性能差距。

Conclusion: 将真实部署会话转化为受控评估实例，为更好衡量智能体在实际使用中的能力提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks should reflect what users actually ask deployed agents to do, yet existing benchmarks often miss key realism properties of real developer-agent sessions. We introduce RealClawBench, a live benchmark framework built from real OpenClaw sessions to capture the distribution, diversity, and real-world difficulty of deployed agent use. Real user requests are challenging to benchmark because they often depend on local execution environments, involve implicit or underspecified intent, and require nontrivial verification. RealClawBench addresses these challenges with two core mechanisms: reconstructed execution environments and deterministic verifiable scorers, which together convert real sessions into reproducible, automatically scored tasks. The resulting release contains 281 executable tasks sampled from a much larger real-session pool while preserving the source distribution, with maximum final-vs-source Jensen-Shannon divergence of 0.0448. Evaluating 14 contemporary models shows that the best system solves only 65.8% of tasks, revealing substantial headroom on realistic developer-agent workloads. By turning real deployed sessions into controlled evaluation instances, RealClawBench provides a practical path toward benchmarks that better measure agent capability in actual use. Code is available at:https://anonymous.4open.science/r/real-claw-bench-582B.

</details>


### [84] [Synthesize and Reward -- Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Step Tool Use in Live Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03892)
*Ibrahim Abdelaziz,Asim Munawar,Kinjal Basu,Maxwell Crouse,Chulaka Gunasekara,Suneet Katrekar,Pavan Kapanipathi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PROVE是一个利用程序化奖励和已验证环境来训练LLM进行多步工具调用的框架，在三个基准测试上取得了最高+10.2分的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多步工具调用训练面临三个相互关联的障碍：构建真实的状态化执行环境成本高昂、合成训练查询与服务器实际状态脱节（导致生成的工具调用无法执行）、基于回忆的RL奖励会鼓励冗长的工具调用模式。

Method: PROVE提出三个贡献：(1)包含20个状态化MCP服务器（343个工具）的库，支持基于实时执行的RL训练；(2)自动化数据合成pipeline，通过依赖引导的对话模拟生成经验证的多轮工具调用轨迹；(3)多组件程序化奖励机制（渐进有效性评分、依赖感知覆盖度、自适应效率惩罚、工具名称信号、参数值匹配奖励），无需外部评判模型。

Result: 在BFCL Multi-Turn、tau2-bench和T-Eval上分别取得最高+10.2、+6.8和+6.5分的提升，训练了Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B、Qwen2.5-7B、Granite-4.1-8B四个模型。

Conclusion: 紧凑的程序化奖励能够在两个模型家族的多步工具编排任务上带来一致的性能提升，证明了无需外部评判模型即可有效训练LLM进行复杂工具调用。

Abstract: Training LLMs to orchestrate multi-step tool calls is held back by three coupled obstacles: realistic stateful execution environments are costly to build, synthetic training queries are often detached from the server's actual state (so the generated tool calls fail to execute), and recall-based RL rewards incentivize verbose tool-calling patterns. We present PROVE (Programmatic Rewards On Verified Environments), a framework with three contributions: (1) a library of 20 stateful MCP (Model Context Protocol) servers exposing 343 tools, enabling live-execution RL training with session-scoped state isolation; (2) an automated data synthesis pipeline that generates validated multi-turn tool-call trajectories against these servers via dependency-graph-guided conversation simulation grounded in live-sampled server state, so every generated query references entities that actually exist; and (3) a multi-component programmatic reward - graduated validity scoring, dependency-aware coverage, an adaptive efficiency penalty with a complexity-scaled call budget, a tool-name signal, and an argument-value matching bonus - requiring no external judge model. We train four models (Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, Granite-4.1-8B) with GRPO using identical reward hyperparameters and ~13K training examples; only learning rate is tuned per model family from a three-point sweep. On BFCL Multi-Turn, tau2-bench, and T-Eval, PROVE yields improvements of up to +10.2, +6.8, and +6.5 points respectively, demonstrating that a compact programmatic reward yields consistent gains on multi-step tool orchestration across two model families.

</details>


### [85] [Knowledge Editing in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03924)
*Haewon Park,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将"定位-编辑"知识编辑方法从自回归模型迁移到掩码扩散模型，发现两类模型在编辑位置上有共性，但扩散模型在多token编辑上性能严重下降，原因在于编辑未针对部分去噪的中间状态进行优化，并提出简单修正方法恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法（定位-编辑）仅针对自回归模型开发，是否适用于双向建模、迭代去噪生成的掩码扩散模型尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将定位-编辑方法迁移到两个MDM（LLaDA, Dream）和两个ARM（LLaMA, Qwen）上进行对比分析，通过因果追踪定位编辑位置，并提出针对中间状态优化的修正方法。

Result: 两类模型在早期到中间层MLP的最后一个主体token位置存在编辑共性；但在多token目标上，MDM的编辑效果系统性退化，而ARM保持稳定；提出针对中间状态的优化修正后，多token性能显著恢复。

Conclusion: 定位-编辑可迁移至MDM，但需针对扩散模型的迭代去噪特性进行调整，简单优化中间状态即可有效恢复多token编辑性能。

Abstract: Knowledge editing aims to update or correct factual knowledge in a language model. A widely used approach, locate-then-edit, does this in two steps: it first localizes a fact within the model, then edits the weights there. To date, such methods have been developed exclusively on autoregressive models (ARMs). Whether their underlying assumptions hold for masked diffusion models (MDMs), which model text bidirectionally and generate by iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, remains an open question. We address it by transferring locate-then-edit to MDMs and comparing two MDMs (LLaDA, Dream) with two ARMs (LLaMA, Qwen) at matched scale. Our central finding has two parts. First, where an edit is applied transfers across paradigms: causal tracing highlights the same early-to-mid-layer MLP at the last subject token in both, and editing is most effective there. Second, this shared location does not guarantee a shared outcome. Single-token edits succeed in both, but as targets grow longer, editing degrades systematically in the MDMs but not the ARMs. The failure stems from how the edited fact is generated: producing a multi-token target requires passing through partially unmasked intermediate states for which the edit was never optimized. Guided by this diagnosis, we introduce a simple correction that optimizes the edit for these states, substantially restoring multi-token performance.

</details>


### [86] [A Pocket Offline Model for Simultaneous Speech Translation as CUNI Submission to IWSLT 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03948)
*Aziz Sharipov Ortega,Dominik Macháček*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 基于Canary离线直译模型结合AlignAtt策略，实现多语言同步翻译系统，在IWSLT 2026竞赛中表现出高质量、低计算需求和多语言支持的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现高质量的同步语音翻译，同时保持低计算开销和多语言覆盖能力。

Method: 采用离线直译模型Canary，结合当前最先进的AlignAtt策略，实现同步翻译能力。

Result: 在低延迟和高延迟场景下，翻译质量均优于同等规模基线模型；模型仅1B参数，计算需求低；支持25种源语言和25种目标语言。

Conclusion: 所提系统在翻译质量、计算效率和多语言支持方面均具备优势，适用于同步语音翻译任务。

Abstract: We implement simultaneous translation capability with the offline direct speech-to-text translation model Canary, using the state-of-the-art policy AlignAtt, and submit it to IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation Shared task for Czech to English and English to German and Italian.
  The strengths of our system are: (1) high translation quality, outperforming similarly sized baselines both in low- and high-latency regimes in computationally unaware simulations; (2) low computational requirements, as the model has only 1B parameters; (3) multilinguality -- support of 25 source and 25 target languages.

</details>


### [87] [Efficient ASR Training with Conversations that Never Happened](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03957)
*Máté Gedeon,Péter Mihajlik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用LLM生成对话文本再通过TTS合成语音的数据增强流水线，在匈牙利语对话ASR任务中，仅用67小时真实数据+636小时合成数据就超越了2700小时真实数据训练的零样本模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言和特定领域的对话ASR训练数据稀缺，尤其是多说话人、领域匹配的对话数据难以获取。

Method: 构建场景级对话生成流水线：1) LLM生成含参与者元数据的对话脚本；2) 将说话人属性映射到TTS音色；3) 合成多说话人对话语音。评估了5个LLM系列在不同生成策略下的效果。

Result: 合成对话持续提升ASR性能；最大配置（67h真实+636h合成）优于2700h真实数据训练的零样本模型；生成器选择和数据组成对收益影响显著。

Conclusion: LLM生成的对话文本经TTS合成后，是真实对话语料库的有效补充，可用于语音模型训练。

Abstract: Conversational ASR for lower-resource languages and niche domains is limited by the scarcity of domain-matched multi-speaker training data. We propose an augmentation pipeline that generates scenario-level dialogues with participant metadata, maps speaker attributes to TTS voice profiles, and assembles synthesized utterances into speaker-aware simulated conversations. We evaluated five LLM families under single-generator, fixed-budget mixture, and scale-up settings using the same FastConformer-Large training recipe for each one. We ran comprehensive evaluations on the Hungarian BEA-Dialogue benchmark corpus, with the method itself being applicable to any language given the resources for each component. The results show that synthetic conversations consistently improve speech recognition performance, but generator choice and data composition strongly affect the gains. Our largest training configuration, using only 67 hours of real conversations and 636 hours of simulated data, achieves better performance on the evaluation benchmark than a zero-shot model trained on 2700 hours of Hungarian speech. These findings indicate that LLM-generated conversational data synthesized with TTS is a practical complement to real conversational corpora for speech model training.

</details>


### [88] [Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering for Efficient and Controllable LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03965)
*Yu Xia,Zhouhang Xie,Xin Xu,Byungkyu Kang,Prarit Lamba,Xiang Gao,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ACTS提出一种基于强化学习的智能体控制方法，通过预算感知的策略引导，在冻结的推理模型上实现高效、可控的链式思考推理，在保持精度的同时大幅节省令牌开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过扩展链式思考提升推理准确性，但存在令牌使用效率低、推理过程难以控制的问题。现有方法通过缩短、提前停止或压缩推理轨迹来提升效率，但无法显式控制模型"如何思考"。

Method: 将推理控制建模为马尔可夫决策过程：控制器智能体在每一步观察当前推理轨迹和剩余思考预算，输出一个包含推理策略和引导短语的转向动作；控制器先通过多预算增强的合成转向轨迹初始化，再使用预算条件奖励塑形的强化学习进一步优化。

Result: 在多个基准上，ACTS在保持与完整推理相当精度的同时大幅节省令牌，并能跨不同推理器和任务实现可控制的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: ACTS通过显式的推理转向控制，实现了高效且可控的链式思考推理，为推理效率与准确性之间的平衡提供了一种灵活的框架。

Abstract: Large language models improve final-answer accuracy through extended chain-of-thought reasoning, but often spend tokens inefficiently and offer little inference-time control. Existing efficient reasoning methods control thinking length by shortening, early-stopping, or compressing traces, leaving how the model thinks implicit. In this paper, we propose Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering (ACTS), which formulates reasoning steering as a Markov decision process where a controller agent adaptively steers a frozen reasoner during inference. At each step, the controller observes the reasoning trace and remaining thinking budget, then issues a steering action consisting of a reasoning strategy and a steering phrase that initiates the next reasoner step. This enables budget-aware strategy control for efficient reasoning while preserving the reasoner's generation continuity. We initialize the controller agent from our constructed synthetic steering trajectories with multi-budget augmentation, and further optimize it via reinforcement learning with budget-conditioned reward shaping. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that ACTS matches full-thinking performance with substantial token savings, and enables controllable accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across different reasoners and tasks. The code is available at https://github.com/Andree-9/ACTS.

</details>


### [89] [AlignAtt4LLM: Fast AlignAtt for Decoder-Only LLMs at IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03967)
*Quentin Fuxa,Dominik Macháček*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AlignAtt4LLM是IWSLT 2026的英德、英意、英中间步语音翻译系统，采用同步级联架构（Qwen3-ASR + Gemma-4 E4B-it），首次将AlignAtt策略应用于仅解码器LLM，通过显式源文本跨度、离线对齐头选择、选择性qk快速重放和运行时查询/键捕获四项创新实现，在低时延（~2秒）和高时延（<4秒）下优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前AlignAtt仅用于编码器-解码器架构，而仅解码器LLM缺乏跨注意力机制，无法直接应用该策略。本文旨在将AlignAtt适配到仅解码器LLM，实现同步语音翻译。

Method: (1) 在提示词中显式标注源文本跨度；(2) 离线选择翻译特定的对齐注意力头；(3) 选择性qk快速重放草稿到源文本的注意力块；(4) 运行时查询/键捕获以比特级一致保留模型输出。

Result: 在IWSLT 2026开发集上，英德和英意方向在低时延（~2秒）和高时延（<4秒）均优于基线；英中结果较混合。方法不限于Gemma-4，可迁移至更强翻译解码器。

Conclusion: 首次成功将AlignAtt应用于仅解码器LLM，提出四项关键技术，在欧语方向表现优异，方法具有通用性。

Abstract: We describe AlignAtt4LLM, an IWSLT 2026 simultaneous speech translation system for English to German, Italian, and Chinese. The system is a synchronous cascade: Qwen3-ASR with forced alignment produces an incrementally updated source transcript, and Gemma-4 E4B-it translates that prefix under an MT-side AlignAtt policy.
  To our knowledge, this is the first application of AlignAtt to a decoder-only LLM, where the encoder-decoder cross-attention used by earlier AlignAtt systems is absent. We recover a usable policy by proposing (1) an explicit source span in the prompt, (2) offline selection of translation-specific alignment heads, (3) selective qk-fast replay of the draft-to-source attention block, and (4) runtime query/key capture that preserves model outputs bit-identically.
  On the IWSLT 2026 development set, AlignAtt4LLM outperforms the supplied baselines for the European target languages, English to German and English to Italian, in both the low-latency regime around 2 seconds and the high-latency regime below 4 seconds CU-LongYAAL. Results for English to Chinese are more mixed, but the method is not tied to Gemma-4: because AlignAtt4LLM only requires a deterministic prompt layout, calibrated attention heads, and query/key capture, the same policy can be reapplied to stronger translation-focused decoder-only MT backbones for non-European target languages.

</details>


### [90] [QUBRIC: Co-Designing Queries and Rubrics for RL Beyond Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03968)
*Rongzhi Zhang,Rui Feng,Zhihan Zhang,Jingfeng Yang,Qingyu Yin,Xin Liu,Zixuan Zhang,Priyanka Nigam,Bing Yin,Tuo Zhao,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QUBRIC是一个通过协同设计查询（queries）和评分标准（rubrics）来改进基于评分标准的强化学习（Rubric-based RL）的框架。它通过教师提取关键点、对比式评分生成和可学习性过滤，在ArenaHard上比SFT基线提升+5.5分，并能迁移到法律、道德和叙事推理等下游任务（平均+6.3分），证明了协同设计查询和评分标准可以使基于评分标准的RL成为RLVR的实用补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的强化学习方法将查询分布视为固定不变，导致评分标准质量受查询结构限制：开放式查询产生模糊的评分标准，而盲目缩小查询范围会引入模型无法验证的虚构引用，导致训练信号失效。作者希望解决这一结构瓶颈，使基于评分标准的RL能够超越严格可验证任务。

Method: QUBRIC包含三个核心步骤：1) 教师提取关键点，将开放式查询重写为基于场景的、可评估的问题；2) 对比式评分标准生成，将教师-策略之间的差距转化为查询级别的评估标准；3) 可学习性过滤，仅保留信息量高的查询-评分标准对用于GRPO训练。

Result: 在ArenaHard上相比SFT基线提升+5.5分。仅使用指令遵循数据训练，即可迁移到三个保留基准（法律、道德、叙事推理），平均提升+6.3分，改进主要集中在推理相关维度。

Conclusion: 协同设计查询和评分标准可以使基于评分标准的强化学习成为RLVR（基于可验证奖励的强化学习）在超越严格可验证任务之外的实用补充，为扩展RL的应用范围提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Rubric-based RL is a promising route for extending reinforcement learning beyond verifiable rewards, yet existing methods optimize rubrics while treating the query distribution as fixed. We identify a structural bottleneck: rubric quality is constrained by query structure. Open-ended queries yield vague rubrics; naively narrowing them introduces fabricated references that no model can verify, so all responses fail and training receives no reward signal. We present QUBRIC, a framework that co-designs queries and rubrics. Teacher-derived key points ground the rewriting of open-ended queries into scenario-based, evaluable questions. Contrastive rubric generation then turns teacher-policy gaps into query-level criteria, and learnability filtering retains only informative query-rubric pairs for GRPO training. QUBRIC achieves a +5.5 point gain on ArenaHard over the SFT baseline. Trained only on instruction-following data, it further transfers to three held-out benchmarks spanning legal, moral, and narrative reasoning (+6.3 points on average), with improvements concentrated in reasoning-related dimensions. These results provide evidence that co-designing queries and rubrics can make rubric-based RL a practical complement to RLVR beyond strictly verifiable tasks.

</details>


### [91] [Quantifying Faithful Confidence Expression in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03969)
*Areeb Gani,Asal Meskin,Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型推理模型（LRMs）在语言表达置信度与内在不确定性之间的忠实校准问题，发现LRMs在信心表达上存在显著不忠实现象，推理行为并不能自动改善校准，且不同置信度估计方法对同一推理轨迹会产生分歧评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的不确定性沟通对于LLMs的可信度至关重要，但忠实校准（FC）——模型内在置信度与语言表达置信度之间的一致性——是一个持续存在的失败模式。随着LRMs的广泛使用，其推理轨迹常被用户解读为深思熟虑和自信的表现，然而LRMs能否忠实地表达其置信度仍然缺乏理解。此外，现有FC评估方法难以推广到LRMs的长思维链输出中。

Method: 提出一个系统量化LRMs忠实校准的框架，从token概率、隐藏状态和采样响应一致性三个来源分析语言决断性与内在不确定性之间的关系，并设计前缀条件采样方法控制推理轨迹中的条件和结构变异。

Result: 忠实置信度表达对LRMs是显著挑战；推理行为不会自动改善FC；针对非推理模型的提示干预在推理设置中无法提升忠实性；不同置信度估计器对同一轨迹产生分歧评估，揭示了先前评估方法的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 该工作将忠实校准确立为LRMs的一个独特的可靠性与对齐目标，特别是在这些系统日益部署于高风险场景的背景下。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty communication is critical to the trustworthiness of LLMs, yet faithful calibration (FC)--the alignment between models' intrinsic and (linguistically) expressed confidence--is a persistent failure mode. This challenge is key for large reasoning models (LRMs), whose extended reasoning traces are often interpreted by users as evidence of deliberation, competence, and confidence. Despite the importance of FC and wide usage of LRMs, the extent to which LRMs can faithfully express their confidence remains poorly understood. Moreover, the prevailing paradigm to measure FC does not generalize well to the long chain-of-thought outputs generated by LRMs, which tend to lack clear step boundaries, involve inconsistent step structure, and encode complex conditional dependencies throughout the trace--complicating estimation of intrinsic confidence. To address this challenge, we introduce a novel framework to systematically quantify FC of LRMs. Our framework analyzes linguistic decisiveness relative to three sources of internal uncertainty, based on token probabilities, hidden states, and sampled response consistency. We also devise a prefix-conditioned sampling approach to control for conditional and structural variation across traces. Applying our framework to a diverse suite of leading models, datasets, and prompts, we find that faithful confidence expression is a significant challenge for LRMs. Reasoning behaviors do not automatically translate to improved FC, and prompt interventions for non-reasoning models do not improve faithfulness in the reasoning setting. Different confidence estimators further produce divergent assessments of the same traces, revealing fragility in prior evaluation methodologies. Taken together, our work establishes FC as a distinct reliability and alignment target for LRMs, particularly as such systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes contexts.

</details>


### [92] [Language Models Compare Quantities Using Number-specific and Unit-specific Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03982)
*Mutsumi Sasaki,Go kamoda,Ryosuke Takahashi,Kosuke Sato,Kentaro Inui,Keisuke Sakaguchi,Benjamin Heinzerling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现语言模型在比较带单位的数量时，并非进行精确的单位换算，而是依赖数值和单位尺度的启发式策略，导致在比较边界附近出现系统性错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型如何处理带测量单位的数量比较任务，特别是模型是否能够像人类一样进行准确的单位换算，还是依赖简化的启发式策略。

Method: 在受控设置下测试语言模型对多种单位系统的数量比较能力，使用线性替代模型预测LM的偏好，并通过因果干预方法验证数值差异和单位尺度差异对模型输出的影响。

Result: 模型在比较边界附近（数值微小变化决定正确答案时）准确率显著下降；错误是系统性的而非随机；线性模型能有效预测LM的比较偏好；对数值和单位尺度相关子空间的因果干预可以改变模型输出。

Conclusion: 语言模型比较带单位数量时，并非先将两者转换为精确的共同尺度表示，而是使用一组关于数值和单位的启发式策略（bag of heuristics）。

Abstract: Quantities with measurement units, such as 110 cm and 1.2 m, require language models (LMs) to combine a numeral with a symbolic unit scale. Here, we study how LMs compare such quantities in controlled settings spanning several unit systems. We find that accuracy degrades near the comparison boundary, where small changes in value determine the correct answer. The resulting errors are systematic: linear surrogate models predict LM preferences from numerical-difference and unit-scale-difference cues, and causal interventions on subspaces aligned with these variables shift model's output. The results suggest that LMs compare quantities through a bag of heuristics over numerals and units, rather than first converting both expressions to an exact shared-scale representation.

</details>

### [93] [Human-in-the-Loop Contextual Bandits for Short-Term Rental Dynamic Pricing: Structural Equivalence of Historical Warm-Up and Approval-Gated Live Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02595)
*Oleg Miroshnichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HITL-GB框架，通过人类介入的上下文赌博机算法解决短租市场动态定价问题，发现历史定价数据可等价于在线策略预热数据，将冷启动时间从约150轮压缩至约30轮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短租市场的动态定价面临重大财务风险、需要可解释性，且市场反馈稀疏（每夜仅一个预订结果），纯在线学习算法冷启动周期过长，不切实际。

Method: 提出人类在环门控赌博机（HITL-GB）框架，结合上下文赌博机算法与人类审批机制，利用历史定价数据（先前确定性策略下收集）作为结构等价的在线策略预热数据，并推导正则化岭回归预热程序。

Result: 在真实短租生产数据（匿名城市市场，2间房，2022.04-2026.04，1461个定价时段）上验证，预热程序将HF-TS系列智能体的有效冷启动从约150轮压缩至约30轮。

Conclusion: 该结构等价结果具有领域无关性，适用于临床用药、信贷审批、内容审核、放射诊断等任何需要人类审批的高风险场景，在受监管行业中，强制人工监督是一种统计资产而非部署约束。

Abstract: Dynamic pricing in short-term rental (STR) markets presents a distinctive challenge for online learning algorithms: pricing decisions carry significant financial risk, operators require explainability, and market feedback is sparse (one booking outcome per listed night). We introduce the Human-in-the-Loop Gated Bandit (HITL-GB) framework, in which a contextual bandit algorithm generates price recommendations but a human agent retains authority to accept, modify, or reject each recommendation before it is applied. We show that under this approval constraint, historical pricing data -- collected under a prior deterministic policy -- is structurally equivalent to on-policy warm-up data for initialising the bandit's posterior, bypassing the weeks-to-months cold-start period that renders pure online bandit learning impractical in sparse-feedback markets. We formalise the approval-gated reward signal, derive a regularised ridge-regression warm-up procedure from historical episodes, and validate the approach on real STR production data (anonymised urban market, 2 rooms, April 2022 -- April 2026, 1,461 nightly pricing episodes). Our warm-up procedure compresses effective cold-start from ~150 episodes to ~30 episodes when initialising agents from the Hierarchical Factored Thompson Sampling (HF-TS) family. We further argue that the structural equivalence result is domain-agnostic: any high-stakes domain where human approval is legally or operationally required -- including clinical drug dosing, credit origination, content moderation, and radiological diagnosis -- satisfies the same conditions and benefits from the same warm-up strategy. In regulated industries, mandatory human oversight is thus a statistical asset rather than a deployment constraint.

</details>


### [94] [Spectral Asymptotics of Neural Network Loss Landscapes: An Exact Decomposition of the Curvature Exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02596)
*Anherutowa Calvo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了谱对齐分解（Spectral Alignment Decomposition），揭示了神经网络中曲率指数α（控制Hessian特征值与梯度奇异值之间的缩放关系）随层类型变化的根本原因。通过将α分解为α=2+d logΦ_k/d logσ_k，将问题转化为几何对齐问题，并推导出谱传递恒等式s=αγ。实验验证了该恒等式在93个层、5种架构和3个数据集上的准确性（中位误差~2%），并展示了基于此设计的Spectral Newton优化器在视觉任务上优于AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络中Hessian-梯度谱关系（曲率指数α）随层类型变化的根本原因，揭示不同架构组件（如卷积、注意力、MLP）之间α值系统差异的几何本质，并利用该理论指导优化器设计。

Method: 提出谱对齐分解（Spectral Alignment Decomposition）：α=2+d logΦ_k/d logσ_k，其中Φ_k衡量Kronecker因子特征基与梯度奇异方向的对齐程度。推导谱传递恒等式s=αγ，连接曲率指数α、有效梯度秩衰减γ和Hessian衰减指数s。通过zeta函数对参与比（participation ratio）进行界分析。

Result: 在93个层、5种架构和3个数据集上，谱传递恒等式s=αγ的中位误差约2%。曲率集中到每层约一个方向。基于此设计的Spectral Newton优化器在α≈2的视觉任务上优于AdamW。

Conclusion: 神经网络的曲率指数α变化可归结为特征基与梯度方向的几何对齐问题；谱传递恒等式提供了一个无自由参数的精确预测框架；基于该理论的自适应预条件优化器（Spectral Newton）在实际任务中具有优势。

Abstract: The curvature exponent $α$ in $h_k \propto σ_k^α$ -- governing how Hessian eigenvalues scale with gradient singular values -- varies systematically across layer types ($α\approx 2$ for convolutions, $\approx 1$ for transformer attention, $< 1$ for MLP up-projections). Why? We prove the Spectral Alignment Decomposition: $α= 2 + d\logΦ_k / d\logσ_k$, where $Φ_k$ measures alignment between Kronecker factor eigenbases and gradient singular directions. This reduces "why does $α$ vary?" to a geometric question we answer for LayerNorm, residual connections, and softmax heads. The decomposition implies a spectral transfer identity $s = αγ$ linking curvature exponent, effective gradient rank-decay $γ$, and Hessian decay exponent $s$. The identity is algebraic; its empirical content is that $α$ and $γ$, fit on independent data (HVPs vs. SVD), recover $s$ to ~2% median error across 93 layers, five architectures, and three datasets -- with no free parameters. A zeta-function bound on participation ratio shows curvature concentrates onto effectively one direction per layer. As a proof of concept, we derive the architecture-adaptive preconditioner $T(σ;α)$ and show that Spectral Newton -- implementing $T$ in the gradient singular basis -- outperforms AdamW on vision benchmarks where $α\approx 2$.

</details>


### [95] [Making Brain-Computer Interfaces More Secure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02597)
*Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Gahangir Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级CNN架构，用于提升EEG-based BCI系统在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，实验表明该模型在分类性能和抗干扰能力上优于EEGNet、DeepConvNet和SleepEEGNet等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG-based BCI研究主要关注分类精度，忽视了安全性和鲁棒性。近期研究发现EEG-based BCI易受对抗攻击，因此评估模型对抗鲁棒性对于可靠部署至关重要。

Method: 提出一种轻量级自定义CNN架构，在梯度对抗攻击场景下，使用两个EEG数据集进行评估，并与EEGNet、DeepConvNet、SleepEEGNet三种专为EEG设计的新型CNN模型进行对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提模型在对抗扰动下的分类性能持续优于基线模型，展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 轻量级架构在提升EEG-based BCI系统在对抗条件下的可靠性方面具有潜力。

Abstract: The development of brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) based on electroencephalograms (EEGs) has advanced significantly mainly to machine learning. Although the majority of earlier research has been on increasing classification accuracy, relatively little focus has been placed on security and robustness. According to recent research, EEG-based BCIs are susceptible to adversarial attacks, which can cause misdiagnosis due to minute, well-crafted disturbances. Evaluating model robustness against such perturbations is therefore critical for ensuring reliable deployment. In this study, we propose a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) architecture to investigate adversarial robustness in EEG-based BCIs. The suggested method is assessed using two EEG datasets and contrasted with three novel CNN models tailored to EEG, namely EEGNet, DeepConvNet, and SleepEEGNet, under gradient-based adversarial attack scenarios. According to experimental findings, the suggested model continuously performs better in classification under adversarial perturbations compared to baseline models, indicating improved robustness. These findings highlight the potential of lightweight architectures for enhancing the reliability of EEG-based BCI systems under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [96] [Assessing Region-Level EEG Contributions to Cognitive Workload Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02598)
*Jacob Wong,Sohan Singh,Prannaya Gupta,Jin Xing Ang,Kritika Johari,U-Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于区域级EEG特征的工作负荷预测评估框架，通过对四个公开数据集的大规模分析发现，额叶电极组在混合被试和独立被试评估中均优于全脑电极基线（相对排名提升15-20%），且额中央区预测稳定性最高，后部和枕部区域贡献不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG广泛用于工作负荷评估，但不同任务、数据集和被试间区域级EEG贡献的一致性尚不明确，缺乏统一的区域级评估框架。

Method: 使用解剖学定义的电极区域提取特征，采用模型无关、基于性能的方法量化区域重要性，在混合被试和独立被试两种评估协议下进行，并通过基于排名的策略聚合结果以保证鲁棒性。

Result: 额叶电极组在独立被试评估中相对全脑基线排名提升15-20%；额中央区预测效用最稳定；后部和枕部区域在不同实验条件下贡献不一致。

Conclusion: 工作负荷相关的EEG信息最稳定地保留在额叶和额中央区电极组中，支持设计高效且泛化能力强的EEG工作负荷监测系统。

Abstract: Accurate and generalizable estimation of cognitive workload from electroencephalography (EEG) is critical for human-centered and safety-critical systems. Although EEG is widely used for workload assessment, the consistency of region-level EEG contributions across tasks, datasets, and subjects remains unclear. This paper presents a region-level evaluation framework for EEG-based workload prediction in which models are trained and evaluated using features extracted exclusively from electrodes belonging to anatomically defined scalp regions. We perform a large-scale analysis across four publicly available EEG workload datasets spanning diverse task demands, recording hardware, and electrode montages. Region importance is quantified using a model-agnostic, performance-based approach under both mixed-subject and subject-independent evaluation protocols, with results aggregated using a rank-based strategy to ensure robustness across experimental configurations. Across all datasets and subject-independent evaluations, frontal electrode groups outperform the full-scalp baseline by approximately 15-20% in relative rank position while using substantially fewer electrodes. Fronto-central regions exhibit the most stable predictive utility, whereas posterior and occipital regions contribute less consistently across experimental conditions. These findings indicate that workload-relevant EEG information is most consistently retained within frontal and fronto-central electrode groups, supporting the design of efficient and generalizable EEG-based workload monitoring systems.

</details>


### [97] [Testing the Test: Score-Direction Instability in Class-Split Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02601)
*Alejandro Ascarate,Leo Lebrat,Rodrigo Santa Cruz,Clinton Fookes,Olivier Salvado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了类别分割异常检测评估协议在异常类与正常样本在表示空间中重叠时可能失效的问题，并提出了一种无需训练的邻域类别泄露诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于类别分割的异常检测评估在异常类与正常样本表示重叠时可能变得病态，导致分数失效甚至反转。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的邻域类别泄露（neighborhood class leakage）诊断方法，在多个数据集和表示空间中进行验证。

Result: 该方法能够预测分数方向的稳定性问题，并在Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10和Imagenette上得到验证。

Conclusion: 类别分割的异常检测基准应被视为几何依赖的压力测试，而非无条件的异常检测能力证据。

Abstract: Within-dataset class-split evaluation is widely used as a proxy for fully unconditional out-of-distribution anomaly detection. We show that this protocol can become ill-posed when the held-out anomaly class overlaps the normal mixture in representation space. In this regime, anomaly scores may collapse toward chance or even invert, and the preferred score direction can depend on the unknown anomaly class. We introduce a simple training-free diagnostic, neighborhood class leakage, and show that it predicts score-direction instability across Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Imagenette, in both pixel and VAE latent spaces. Our results suggest that class-split AD benchmarks should be treated as geometry-dependent stress tests rather than unconditional evidence of anomaly-detection ability.

</details>


### [98] [Graph Mamba Survival Analysis Based on Topology-Aware ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02602)
*Yuanfang Chen,Peiqiang Yan,Yuntao Shou,Qian Zhao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TopoMamSurv框架，通过拓扑感知排序(TAO)、双向Mamba模块和图卷积网络(GCN)的层次化特征学习，解决了WSI生存分析中Transformer计算瓶颈、Mamba序列敏感性和单向架构问题，在五个TCGA数据集上取得优势性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI生存分析面临Transformer的O(N²)计算瓶颈、Mamba对输入顺序高度敏感（传统排序方法忽略拓扑连接）、以及Mamba单向架构无法利用图像双向空间结构等挑战，亟需一种兼顾长程依赖建模、计算效率和空间结构利用的方法。

Method: 提出TopoMamSurv框架：1) 拓扑感知排序(TAO)策略，使节点排序适应Mamba的序列敏感性；2) 双向Mamba模块，实现双向空间上下文建模；3) 集成GCN形成"局部聚合-全局捕获"的层次化特征学习架构。

Result: 可视化实验证明TAO策略提取的节点具有更高相似性；在五个TCGA数据集上验证了框架的全面性能优势。

Conclusion: TopoMamSurv通过TAO、双向语义建模和层次特征融合的系统设计，有效调和了WSI分析中长程依赖建模、计算效率与空间结构利用之间的矛盾。

Abstract: In computational pathology, Whole Slide Images (WSIs) survival analysis is crucial for patient prognosis assessment, but it faces multiple technical challenges. Although the Transformer captures long-range dependencies through its self-attention mechanism, its $O(N^2)$ time complexity causes a severe computational bottleneck in large-scale WSIs graph structures. The Mamba model breaks through the Transformer's computational bottleneck with linear complexity. But, owing to Mamba's high sensitivity to the order of input data, traditional node sorting methods in Graph Mamba, such as those based on node degree or subgraph size, fail to adequately account for the topological connectivity of graph data. This inadequacy consequently restricts the performance of Mamba's sequential modeling. Moreover, its unidirectional architecture cannot leverage the bidirectional spatial structure of images. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel Graph Mamba survival analysis framework based on topology-aware ordering (TopoMamSurv) to adapt to the sequential sensitivity of Mamba. Our visualization experiments further confirmed that the nodes extracted through the topology-aware ordering (TAO) strategy indeed exhibit higher similarity. Furthermore, we designed a bidirectional Mamba module and integrated a Graph Convolutional Network (GCN) to achieve bidirectional spatial context modeling of images, forming a hierarchical feature learning architecture for "local aggregation - global capture." This framework effectively reconciles the contradiction between long-range dependency modeling, computational efficiency, and spatial structure utilization in WSIs analysis through its systematic design of TAO, bidirectional semantic modeling, and hierarchical feature fusion. This framework has been validated for its comprehensive performance advantage on five TCGA datasets.

</details>


### [99] [Auditable Climate Risk Intelligence from Fragmented ESG Data: Deterministic Orchestration and Imbalance-Aware Learning for Scope 1-3 Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02604)
*Karan Sehgal,Khawar Naveed Bhatti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个确定性气候风险智能框架，整合单一真实源编排、时序异常检测、不平衡集成学习与可解释治理，用于可审计的ESG验证，并构建了合成ESG验证基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESG与气候风险数据在范围1、2、3报告环境中高度碎片化，传统验证管道缺乏溯源感知的可审计性、隐蔽漂移检测和面向可复现性的治理机制。

Method: 框架结合时序漂移分析、SMOTE稀有事件优化、集成学习、溯源感知编排及TreeSHAP可解释性，在合成ESG基准数据集上进行分类指标、校准指标和治理导向的审计追踪完整性评估。

Result: 与统计分类器、异常检测方法、时序预测基线和阈值系统相比，所提框架在召回率、F1、ROC AUC、ECE、Brier评分及溯源链重建完整性指标上表现更优，结果经五折分层交叉验证与配对显著性检验。

Conclusion: 该框架将ESG报告重新定义为确定性气候风险治理基础设施，支持可复现性、可解释性和操作性审计能力。

Abstract: ESG and climate risk data remain fragmented across heterogeneous Scope 1, Scope 2, and Scope 3 reporting environments, while conventional validation pipelines lack provenance aware auditability, hidden drift detection, and reproducibility oriented governance. This paper proposes a deterministic climate risk intelligence framework integrating single source of truth orchestration, temporal anomaly detection, imbalance aware ensemble learning, and explainability oriented governance for auditable ESG validation. To support open reproducibility, we construct and release a synthetic ESG validation benchmark calibrated against publicly reported characteristics of the GHG Protocol, PCAF, and ISSB standards. The methodology incorporates temporal drift analysis, SMOTE based rare event optimization, ensemble learning, provenance aware orchestration, and TreeSHAP based interpretability for governance inspection and audit reconstruction. We evaluate the framework against statistical classifiers, anomaly detection methods, temporal forecasting baselines, and a threshold based system using classification metrics (recall, F1, ROC AUC), calibration metrics (ECE, Brier score), and a governance oriented audit trace completeness metric measuring the fraction of flagged anomalies for which a deterministic source to escalation provenance chain can be reconstructed. Results are reported as mean and standard deviation across stratified five fold cross validation with paired significance testing. The framework reframes ESG reporting toward deterministic climate risk governance infrastructure supporting reproducibility, explainability, and operational auditability.

</details>


### [100] [Cross-Modal Contrastive Learning of ECG and Angiography Representations for Severe Stenosis Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02605)
*Nikola Cenikj,Özgün Turgut,Alexander Müller,Alexander Steger,Jan Kehrer,Marcus Brugger,Daniel Rueckert,Philip Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出StenCE预训练框架，利用心电图（ECG）数据检测冠状动脉狭窄，特别适用于无症状患者。实验表明，该方法在不同狭窄严重程度阈值和多种心电图编码器上均优于现有方法，首次在严重狭窄分类中取得高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前冠状动脉狭窄诊断依赖有创、耗时、昂贵的冠状动脉造影，导致部分无症状患者可能漏诊。心电图虽快捷、廉价、无创且常用于无症状患者，但缺乏可靠的狭窄特异性信号，无法用于狭窄风险分层。

Method: 提出StenCE预训练框架，从心电图中提取特征，实现对患者进行狭窄风险分层，并支持不同的心电图编码器。

Result: 在不同狭窄严重程度阈值和额外心电图疾病分类任务上，StenCE均一致地提升了多种心电图编码器的性能，优于先前工作，并首次在严重狭窄分类中取得高性能。

Conclusion: StenCE成功检测到心电图中用于狭窄诊断的信号，为无症状患者的早期筛查提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Coronary artery stenosis is a common cardiovascular disease, with severe, untreated cases posing significant risks of heart attack. Although coronary (X-ray) angiograms remain the standard for stenosis diagnosis, they are invasive, time- and resource-intensive, and therefore only performed on patients with a high probability of disease based on symptoms and prior clinical tests. However, a subset of patients, especially those without symptoms, may remain undiagnosed. Detecting indications of stenosis from ECGs, which are fast, cheap, non-invasive, and thus routinely acquired even in asymptomatic patients, would support early diagnosis. However, as no reliable stenosis-specific signal has been identified in ECGs, they can not currently be used for stenosis risk stratification. To address this, we introduce StenCE, a pretraining framework, allowing stratification of patients based on features derived directly from ECGs. Evaluations across varying stenosis severity thresholds and additional ECG disease classification tasks demonstrate consistent performance improvements across different ECG encoders, outperforming previous work. The obtained models successfully detect signals for stenosis diagnosis in ECGs and are the first to achieve high performance in severe stenosis classification. The source code is available at https://github.com/NikolaCenic/ecg-stenosis-cls.

</details>


### [101] [ReLoRA: Knowledge-Reusing Adaptation for Fast Rollout of Evolving LLM Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02606)
*Yang Xu,Zihuai Xu,Hongli Xu,Yunming Liao,Zhiwei Yao,Xitong Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReLoRA框架，通过贝叶斯优化初始化和分阶段正则化微调，高效恢复LLM基座模型更新后LoRA适配器的服务质量，实现8.9倍加速和4.6%精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为持续演进的服务，基座模型频繁更新会导致之前部署的任务特定LoRA适配器失效。重新训练所有适配器计算成本高且延迟服务部署，而直接复用则因适配器与骨干网络不兼容而导致服务质量下降。

Method: ReLoRA包含两步优化：1)自适应LoRA初始化——利用贝叶斯优化融合旧适配器信息和基座模型演进信息，构建兼容性感知的起始点；2)带调度正则化的微调——先通过强正则化快速将适配器引导到高质量区域，再放松正则化进行任务特定精调。

Result: 相比基线方法，ReLoRA将恢复时间加速最高8.9倍，精度提升最高4.6%。

Conclusion: ReLoRA能在基座模型更新场景下高效恢复LoRA适配器的服务质量，显著降低重新适配开销，同时保持甚至提升任务性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as continuously evolving services, where frequent base-model updates may invalidate previously deployed task-specific Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapters. For service providers managing numerous downstream model services, retraining each LoRA adapter from scratch for every updated base model is computationally prohibitive and delays service rollout. Meanwhile, the simpler alternative, i.e., naively applying the original LoRA adapter to the updated base model, often leads to degraded service quality due to adapter-backbone incompatibility. To address this problem, we propose ReLoRA, a knowledge-reusing re-adaptation framework that efficiently restores service-ready LoRA adapters for evolving LLM services while preserving or improving task performance. Specifically, ReLoRA comprises two key optimization steps: 1) Adaptive LoRA initialization leverages Bayesian optimization to construct a compatibility-aware starting point by fusing information from both the previously deployed task adapter and the base model's evolution; 2) Fine-tuning with scheduled regularization first rapidly steers the adapter to a high-quality region via strong regularization, followed by relaxed regularization for task-specific refinement. This design enables rapid service-quality recovery with reduced re-adaptation overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReLoRA reduces time-to-readiness by up to 8.9$\times$ and improves accuracy by up to 4.6\% compared to baselines.

</details>


### [102] [Geometry-Aware Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02607)
*David Turtora Zagardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何感知表格扩散模型（GATD），通过向扩散去噪器注入列间角度和长度等几何关系作为输入和辅助目标，在仅用平均3.5倍更少参数的情况下，在10个数据集上取得8/10形状、7/10趋势和9/10下游效用的SOTA结果，形状和趋势误差分别降低27%和20%。消融实验证明监督信号（而非额外输入或容量）是性能提升的关键，且该归纳偏置可迁移至GNN和Transformer架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格扩散模型依赖隐式机制捕捉列间关系，缺乏显式结构化引导，导致生成质量受限。

Method: 计算列值差异的成对角度和长度，将其作为扩散去噪器的额外输入和辅助训练目标，并在MLP、GNN、Transformer等多种架构上验证。

Result: MLP版本以平均3.5倍更少参数在10个数据集上取得8/10形状、7/10趋势、9/10下游效用最优；形状和趋势误差分别降低27%和20%；几何监督可迁移至其他架构，改善27/30的形状和25/30的趋势。

Conclusion: 显式关系监督是一种可移植的表格扩散归纳偏置，其增益主要来自监督信号本身而非额外输入或模型容量。

Abstract: Tabular synthesis is critical for privacy-preserving sharing and augmentation, yet diffusion models rely on implicit mechanisms to capture inter-column relationships. We introduce Geometry-Aware Tabular Diffusion (GATD), which augments tabular diffusion denoisers with pairwise angles and lengths computed from column value differences and used as inputs and auxiliary targets. Our MLP instantiation achieves state-of-the-art benchmark performance while using 3.5x fewer parameters on average (up to 25x for classification tasks): on ten datasets, it wins 8/10 Shape, 7/10 Trend, and 9/10 downstream utility (F1/RMSE), reducing Shape and Trend error by 27% and 20%. Default loss weights transfer to GNN and Transformer denoisers, improving Shape on 27/30 and Trend on 25/30 architecture-dataset cells. A matched ablation shows supervision (not extra inputs or capacity) drives the gain. This shows explicit relational supervision is a portable inductive bias for tabular diffusion.

</details>


### [103] [Pruning Deep Neural Networks via the Marchenko--Pastur Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02608)
*Leonid Berlyand,Theo Bourdais,Houman Owhad,Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Marchenko–Pastur随机矩阵理论的剪枝方法，能够在极短微调预算下保留深度神经网络精度，并在ImageNet-1k上实现多种架构的高稀疏比加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度神经网络剪枝方法通常需要长程重优化流程，微调预算高。本文旨在实现短校准与微调周期下的精度保持，降低剪枝后的再训练成本。

Method: 利用Marchenko–Pastur随机矩阵理论分析剪枝影响，给出确定性数据路径保证：当移除分量R的传播logit效应足够小时，剪枝会降低弹性网络目标并保留密集裕度超过两倍扰动的样本。扩展了零预算完美剪枝、剪枝-恢复固定稀疏模式、以及L2正则化模型。在iid高斯充分条件下，MP边缘σ⁺提供逐层预算信号。

Result: ImageNet-1k上，ViT-B/16 2:4+ToMe经3轮蒸馏达83.41% top-1，稀疏执行MAC降低59.81%，A40加速1.388倍；无ToMe的A100端点达2.705倍。结构化稀疏下ViT-B/16 6:12达83.74%，ViT-L/16 8:16达85.33%，ConvNeXtV2-Base 12:16达86.35%。ResNet50 8:16达75.87%，ResNet152d CAST-conv+permutation达81.33%，约50% MAC节省，A40加速1.62倍。

Conclusion: 基于MP随机矩阵理论的剪枝方法能在极短微调预算下有效保留精度，并实现显著的结构化与非结构化稀疏加速，为实际部署提供了理论与实验支持。

Abstract: We study a Marchenko--Pastur (MP) random-matrix approach to pruning deep neural networks with very small post-pruning fine-tuning budgets. The main practical contribution is accuracy retention under short calibration and fine-tuning schedules, rather than a long post-pruning reoptimization pipeline. The theory gives deterministic data-path certificates: if the removed component $R$ has small propagated logit effect $L_s \| R ψ_1(s) \|_\infty$, pruning decreases an elastic-net objective and preserves samples whose dense margin exceeds twice the perturbation. The zero-budget case gives perfect pruning; a prune--restore extension models weight restoration inside a fixed sparse-execution pattern; and an additive $L_2$-regularized model shows admissible random-like components vanish at the training limit, with persistent spikes stabilizing as the MP bulk collapses. Under iid-Gaussian sufficient conditions, the fitted MP edge $σ_+$ gives a high-probability layerwise budget signal.
  On ImageNet-1k, after only three distillation epochs, ViT-B/16 $2{:}4{+}$ToMe reaches $83.41\%$ top-1 ($-1.70$ pp from dense) at $59.81\%$ sparse-execution MAC reduction, with $1.388\times$ best-observed A40 native-$2{:}4$ backend speedup for the same checkpoint and ToMe graph; a separate no-ToMe A100 endpoint gives $2.705\times$. At structured sparsity, ViT-B/16 $6{:}12$ reaches $83.74\%$, ViT-L/16 $8{:}16$ dense+permutation reaches $85.33\%$ ($-0.51$ pp), and ConvNeXtV2-Base $12{:}16$ reaches $86.35\%$ ($-0.37$ pp). For CNNs, ResNet50 $8{:}16$ dense+permutation reaches $75.87\%$ ($-0.26$ pp), and ResNet152d CAST-conv+permutation reaches $81.33\%$ ($-1.53$ pp) at ${\sim}50\%$ MAC accounting with a $1.62\times$ A40 im2col$+2{:}4$ sparse-GEMM audit.

</details>


### [104] [Building Better Activation Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02609)
*Jan Bauer,Celeste De Schamphelaere,Adam Karvonen,Niclas Luick,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过四项改进优化了激活预言机（AO）的训练流程，并开源了首个综合性AO评估套件AObench，为可扩展端到端可解释性奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活预言机存在幻觉、模糊性和文本反转混淆等问题，且难以评估，需要改进训练方法和建立标准化评估体系。

Method: 四项改进：1) 基于策略rollout训练；2) 改进对话数据集；3) 输入更多网络层；4) 优化注入公式。同时开源首个综合评估套件AObench。

Result: 能力提升效果有限（边际改进），但生活质量（可用性）方面的改进较为显著。

Conclusion: 该工作为改进AO及其他模型的可扩展端到端可解释性奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Activation Oracles (AOs) are promising methods for interpreting residual stream activations. However, current AOs face important issues, such as hallucinations and vagueness. Additionally, text-inversion confounds make them hard to evaluate. To this end, we improve the Activation Oracle (AO) training regime in four ways: training on on-policy rollouts, improving the conversational dataset, feeding more layers and an improvement to the injection formula. The capability improvements are marginal, but quality of life improvements are quite substantial. In addition, we open source the first comprehensive evaluation suite for AO quality, which we call AObench. Overall, we hope that our work sets a foundation that helps improve AOs and other models in the paradigm of scalable, end-to-end interpretability.

</details>


### [105] [Hallucination Is Linearly Decodable from Mid-Layer Hidden States in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02628)
*Aizierjiang Aiersilan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了开源大语言模型在隐藏状态中是否编码了线性可分的真实性信号，并在Llama-3.1、Mistral-7B和Qwen2.5三个模型上，使用多种检测方法在四个基准上进行了系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究开源LLM的隐藏状态中是否存在线性可分的真实性信号，并定位该信号在网络深度中的最强位置。

Method: 对三个7B-8B指令微调模型进行4-bit量化，提取每层隐藏状态，在TruthfulQA、HaluEval-QA、FEVER和合成数据集上比较线性探针、MLP探针、INSIDE EigenScore、自一致性方法和注意力熵四种检测方法。

Result: 单层线性探针在中间网络层达到0.904-1.000 AUROC，而基于采样的检测方法不超过0.541 AUROC。MLP探针性能相比线性探针提升不超过0.01 AUROC。注意力熵在知识导向任务上提供0.866-0.941 AUROC的补充信号。

Conclusion: 开源LLM的隐藏状态中存在近似线性的真实性信号，峰值层在不同模型家族中呈现一致带区；采样方法表现差源于结构性不匹配而非方法本身局限。

Abstract: We investigate whether open-source LLMs encode a linearly separable truthfulness signal in their hidden states, and at which network depth this signal is strongest. Across three $7$B--$8$B instruction-tuned models (Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, Qwen2.5-7B) loaded in $4$-bit NF4 quantization, we extract per-layer hidden states on four hallucination benchmarks (TruthfulQA, HaluEval-QA, FEVER, and a controlled synthetic set) and compare four detection approaches: linear and MLP probes, INSIDE EigenScore, self-consistency, and attention entropy. A linear probe on a single mid-network layer achieves $0.904$--$1.000$ AUROC on held-out splits, while sampling-based detectors do not exceed $0.541$ AUROC under the same protocol. The truthfulness signal is approximately linear: MLP probes rarely surpass linear probes by more than $0.01$ AUROC. Peak probing layers fall in a consistent band across model families on natural-language benchmarks -- blocks~$13$--$18$ of~$32$ for Llama and Mistral, and blocks~$19$--$25$ of~$28$ for Qwen. First-block attention entropy provides a complementary signal in knowledge-grounded settings ($0.866$--$0.941$ AUROC on HaluEval-QA) at no additional inference cost. The low discriminability of sampling methods under this protocol reflects a structural mismatch between paired-label evaluation and the information these methods access, rather than an inherent limitation of those methods. Code and data are released for full reproducibility on a single $8$\,GB GPU.

</details>


### [106] [Regime-Arrival Uncertainty in Generalization Bounds under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02657)
*Prince Poudel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在马尔可夫切换环境下量化分布偏移导致额外风险的框架，并给出了泛化误差的精确分解，证明仅靠训练数据无法预测危机的时间到来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准泛化界假设训练与部署分布相同或静态，未考虑市场在平静与危机状态之间切换的体制转换环境。

Method: 通过马尔可夫切换模型量化体制组成不匹配带来的额外风险；利用谱间隙校正的有效样本量将界限扩展到β-混合数据；给出极小化下界。

Result: 提出的惩罚项是事后实现的泛化差距，而仅基于训练数据的估计器没有显著相关性；可以检测到危机的特征几何结构，但无法预测时间到来。

Conclusion: 该框架不是预测机器，未来体制组成的预测在体制变化的罕见情况下仍是开放问题。

Abstract: The standard generalization bounds assume that the training and deployment distributions are the same, or are static, and don't consider regime switching environments where the ratio of calm vs crisis states is different. This paper proposes a framework that generalizes regime-aware models by quantifying the extra risk due to regime composition mismatch, when distribution shifts are Markov-switching. We obtain an exact decomposition, separating regime mismatch from regime sensitivity; we extend the bound to beta-mixing data using the effective sample size corrected for the spectral gap; and we show a minimax lower bound for synthetic data and on 25 years of global equity indices. The proposed penalty is an ex post realized generalization gap, whereas the training-only estimator does not show significant correlation: the feature geometry of crises can be detected, but not the temporal arrival. Thus, the framework is not a forecast machine. Forecasting the composition of the future regime is an open question in the rare cases of regime change.

</details>


### [107] [CL-DMDF:Dynamic Multimodal Data Fusion Model Based on Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02659)
*Dong Li,Lingling Zhang,Binghao Han,Linlin Ding,Yue Kou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对比学习的动态多模态数据融合模型（CL-DMDF），通过跨特征和模态维度的注意力机制、实体质心对比学习及自适应融合模块，有效应对现实场景中模态缺失问题，并在三个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多针对完全观测模态设计，难以处理实际应用中模态不确定或缺失的情况，且部分模型过度关注缺失模态的局部交互，忽略了多模态表示中的全局互补线索。

Method: 提出CL-DMDF模型，包含三个核心模块：1）跨特征和模态维度的新型注意力机制，计算可靠的重要性分数；2）实体质心对比学习模块，基于实体特征构建质心正样本增强判别学习；3）自适应融合模块，提升动态融合策略的效率与准确性。

Result: 在三个数据集上的大量实验表明，CL-DMDF在多种多模态融合任务中均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: CL-DMDF通过全局互补线索挖掘和动态融合策略，有效解决了模态缺失场景下的多模态数据融合问题。

Abstract: Multimodal data fusion involves integrating and analyzing information from multiple modalities to uncover latent correlations and complementary patterns, thereby enhancing data processing and decision-making. While existing methods for structured multimodal inputs are typically designed around specific tasks and assume fully observed modalities, real-world applications often suffer from uncertain or missing modality inputs due to various factors. Some traditional models overly emphasize local interactions within missing modalities, neglecting the global complementary cues embedded in multimodal representations. To overcome these limitations, we propose a Dynamic Multimodal Data Fusion model based on Contrastive Learning (CL-DMDF). CL-DMDF introduces a novel attention mechanism that operates across both feature and modality dimensions to compute reliable attention scores, effectively reflecting importance at each level. The CL-DMDF further incorporates an entity-centroid contrastive learning module that constructs centroid-based positive samples from entity features to enhance discriminative learning. Additionally, an adaptive fusion module is employed to improve the efficiency and accuracy of dynamic fusion strategies. Extensive experiments conducted on three datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the CL-DMDF across diverse multimodal fusion tasks.

</details>


### [108] [Improvise, Adapt, Overcome: An On-The-Fly Multifidelity Algorithm for Efficient Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02662)
*Vivin Vinod,Peter Zaspel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应多保真机器学习框架，能够动态决定各保真度训练数据的组成比例，在量子化学计算中实现高达30倍的数据生成成本降低，并减少数据冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多保真机器学习依赖预定义的缩放因子来决定高低保真数据比例，容易产生冗余数据，导致效率损失。因此，需要一种能自动优化训练数据组成的自适应方法。

Method: 提出自适应在线多保真框架（adaptive-MFML），通过动态查询各保真度训练样本，在低保真度下先饱和模型精度，再逐步转向更昂贵的参考计算，自主决定训练数据集组成。

Result: 在耦合簇能量和激发能等化学属性的基准测试中，自适应算法相比单保真方法降低数据生成成本达30倍，相比标准MFML提升达5倍。

Conclusion: 该自适应框架有效缓解了数据冗余问题，为量子化学领域建立了高精度、低成本且可持续的成本感知机器学习路径。

Abstract: Machine learning has accelerated quantum chemistry but is hindered by the prohibitive cost of generating high fidelity training data. Multifidelity machine learning (MFML) mitigates this overhead by systematically combining abundant low fidelity data with sparse high fidelity data. In spite of its success, standard MFML schemes rely on pre-defined scaling factors to determine sparse data ratio across fidelities, often generating redundant multifidelity data resulting in a loss of efficiency. Here, we introduce an adaptive on-the-fly multifidelity framework for machine learning that autonomously determines training dataset composition. By dynamically querying training samples at each fidelity, the algorithm saturates model accuracy at lower fidelities before moving up to more expensive reference calculations. We benchmark the novel adaptive-MFML across diverse chemical properties including the computational chemistry gold standard coupled cluster energies, and the more chemically challenging excitation energies. In our numerical experiments we show that our adaptive algorithm reduces data generation costs by up to a factor of 30 compared to single fidelity methods and improves upon standard MFML by up to a factor of 5. The mitigation of data redundancy establishes a high-accuracy low-cost pathway for sustainable cost-aware machine learning in quantum chemistry.

</details>


### [109] [AdaWeather: Adaptively Mixing Probabilistic Weather Forecasts with Logarithmic Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02663)
*Saptarishi Dhanuka,Sarvesh Iyer,Manmeet Singh,Mihir More,Rushil Gupta,Dhruman Gupta,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Sandeep Juneja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaWeather框架，通过自适应组合多个概率天气预报模型，实现比单一最优模型更好的预测性能，并证明其具有对数遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率天气预报模型在时空上表现不一致，没有单一模型能持续占优，因此需要自适应方法组合多个预测以获得提升和鲁棒性。

Method: AdaWeather结合机器学习与专家混合方法，自适应组合多个概率天气预报；算法和分析扩展到与最优静态专家混合相比具有对数遗憾界。

Result: 在温度预测任务上，AdaWeather相比现有方法取得了更好的预测效果。

Conclusion: 自适应组合多个概率天气预报模型是有效的，AdaWeather的理论保证和实证结果证明了其优越性。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have produced probabilistic weather forecasting models comparable to state-of-the-art numerical weather predictors. But no model consistently dominates spatio-temporally, and relative performance is highly context-dependent. This motivates adaptive methods for combining multiple forecasts to obtain improvements and robustness. While combined forecasts have been proposed in the literature, these are achieved either through supervised learning or through prediction with expert advice methods. We introduce AdaWeather, an adaptive framework that combines many probabilistic forecasts using both machine learning as well as mixture of experts to arrive at a unified improved probabilistic forecast. While traditional expert methods develop the regret bounds with respect to the best single expert in hindsight, we extend the algorithm and analysis to show our method has logarithmic regret compared to the best static mixture of experts in hindsight. Empirically, we focus on forecasting temperature, and observe improvements over existing methods.

</details>


### [110] [Anomalies in Multivariate Time Series Benchmarks Are Mostly Univariate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02670)
*Marc Pinet,Julien Cumin,Samuel Berlemont,Dominique Vaufreydaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过诊断框架发现，当前多变量时间序列异常检测基准数据集中，没有无单变量异常伴随的纯跨通道异常，因此跨通道建模在现有基准上并无实质增益，呼吁构建更具结构多样性的评测集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTSAD模型普遍引入跨通道建模，但这一做法基于“异常结构可能分布在多个通道”的隐含假设，然而该假设在真实基准上是否成立尚未被系统验证。

Method: 设计了一个逐片段诊断框架，对每个标注异常片段检测：(1)是否有至少一个通道偏离其正常历史，(2)跨通道相关性结构是否发生变化，或两者同时发生。同时构建合成数据（相位偏移正弦通道+共享噪声）进行对照验证。

Result: 在8个基准数据集中，没有出现无单变量异常的纯跨通道异常；在6个基准中，至少一半的异常片段有79%-100%的时间步存在单变量偏离；CD模型在合成数据上有增益，但在真实基准上无 measurable gain。

Conclusion: 当前MTSAD基准不适合验证跨通道建模能力，需开发结构更多样的评测集。

Abstract: Many recent multivariate time series anomaly detection (MT-SAD) models incorporate cross-channel modeling, under the implicit assumption that the structure of anomalies may be spread across multiple channels. We evaluate this assumption on eight widely used public benchmarks by introducing a per-segment diagnostic framework that flags, for each labeled anomaly, whether at least one channel deviates individually from its normal history, whether the cross-channel correlation structure changes, or both. The framework shows that no crosschannel rupture occurs without an accompanying univariate deviation across a range of reasonable thresholds. A complementary metric also reveals that on six of the eight benchmarks, at least half of the labeled anomaly segments deviate univariately on 79% to 100% of their timesteps, reaching 100% on three of these datasets. To verify that our framework captures cross-channel structure when present, we construct synthetic data of phase-shifted sinusoidal channels with shared noise. Each anomalous segment is altered through one of two channelwise corruptions that preserve the per-channel marginal distribution while breaking cross-channel structure, and our framework correctly characterizes these segments as cross-channel-only. On these data, channel-dependent (CD) models successfully exploit the cross-channel signal whereas channel-independent (CI) ones fail. The CI/CD comparison of a recent SOTA detector on real benchmarks further confirms that CD modeling brings no measurable gain. We conclude that current MTSAD benchmarks are unsuitable for validating cross-channel modeling capabilities, and we call for the development of more structurally diverse evaluation sets. The code for this study is publicly available.

</details>


### [111] [Aligning Data-Driven Predictors with Allocation: A Decision-Focused Approach to Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02671)
*Itai Zilberstein,Ioannis Anagnostides,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有生存预测模型（如C-index）在器官分配等决策任务中可能导致任意差的效果，并提出基于NDCG的决策焦点学习方法，在心脏移植数据上提升50-100%的NDCG，转化为每年数万生命年收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准统计指标优化的预测模型与下游算法决策任务之间存在严重错配，即使高精度模型也可能在器官分配中产生比随机选择更差的结果，亟需弥合生存分析与策略优化之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出决策焦点学习方法，采用信息检索中的归一化折损累积增益（NDCG）作为优化目标，证明NDCG在生存分析中能保证分配性能；采用bootstrap方法优化现有生存模型的NDCG，并解决右删失数据对排序评估的挑战。

Result: 在美国历史心脏移植数据上，该方法将基线模型的NDCG提升50-100%，转化为每年数万额外生命年收益。

Conclusion: 本框架弥合了预测模型与决策任务之间的错配，有望在更广泛的预测驱动决策场景中得到应用。

Abstract: Machine learning predictors have become essential tools for guiding automated decision making. However, a major misalignment persists: predictive models are typically optimized in terms of standard statistical metrics in isolation from the algorithmic tasks they inform. We highlight this incongruity in the high-stakes domain of organ allocation by demonstrating that any algorithm relying on (even highly accurate) survival predictors optimized for standard metrics -- such as the Concordance index (C-index) -- can yield arbitrarily poor outcomes when used for allocation, failing to guarantee utility better than a uniform random selection. To bridge the gap between survival analysis and policy optimization, we introduce a decision-focused learning approach based on optimizing normalized discounted cumulative gain (NDCG), a mainstay metric in information retrieval. We establish the utility of NDCG in survival analysis by proving that it translates to guarantees on the performance of allocation. Empirically, we propose a bootstrapping approach to optimize the NDCG of existing survival models. Unlike prior work, we also address the challenge of right censorship when evaluating ranking. On historical heart transplant data from the US, our method dramatically boosts the NDCG of baseline models by 50-100%, which translates to tens of thousands of additional life years gained annually when deployed for transplant allocation. We anticipate that our framework will find broader applications in decision making with predictions.

</details>


### [112] [Before Fusion, Ask What to Keep: Contextual Calibration of Multimodal Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02679)
*Jiyuan Liu,Liangwei Nathan Zheng,Wei Emma Zhang,Xinpei Wang,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态校准模块，在融合前对各模态特征进行实例级和维度级的调制，以抑制冲突信息、增强协同信号，在五个基准任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态系统中，不同模态的信息并非总是有益，某些模态可能对特定输入产生干扰，局部特征响应也可能与其他模态证据冲突。现有方法多在融合后才处理这些问题，缺乏在融合前按需调整各模态表示的机制。

Method: 设计一个轻量级校准模块，在摘要层面对各模态进行成对比较，提取跨模态支持与冲突的线索，将其转化为实例级和维度级的调制信号。这些校准信号在融合前作用于原始模态特征，而非作用于已融合的表示。该模块作为即插即用组件，可附加到不同融合骨干网络。

Result: 在情感理解、动作识别、音视频事件检测和音视频情感分类五个基准上，该方法在序列式和卷积式融合设置下均提升了性能。额外分析（模态移除、合成损坏、训练动态、特征可视化）表明，融合前校准能减少不可靠模态的干扰，产生更稳定的多模态优化。

Conclusion: 融合前的多模态校准比融合后处理更有效，能够抑制误导性成分、保留弱但有用的证据、强调当前多模态上下文支持的响应，是一种灵活且有效的通用多模态增强策略。

Abstract: Multimodal systems often benefit from combining information across language, sound, and visual streams, but this benefit is not guaranteed. A modality that is useful for one input may become distracting for another, and local feature responses within the same modality can disagree with evidence from other sources. This work investigates how to adjust multimodal representations before they are merged by a downstream predictor. We develop a compact calibration module that compares each modality with the others at the summary level, extracts cues of cross-source support and conflict, and converts these cues into instance-wise and dimension-wise modulation signals. The calibration is applied to the original modality features rather than to already fused representations, enabling the model to suppress misleading components, preserve weak but useful evidence, and emphasize responses that are better supported by the current multimodal context. The module is designed as a plug-in component and can be attached to different fusion backbones without changing their prediction heads. Across five benchmarks covering sentiment understanding, action recognition, audio-visual event detection, and audio-visual emotion classification, the proposed pre-combination calibration strategy improves performance under both sequence-based and convolutional fusion settings. Additional analyses under modality removal, synthetic corruption, training dynamics, and feature-level visualization show that calibrating signals before fusion can reduce interference from unreliable modalities and produce more stable multimodal optimization.

</details>


### [113] [Locality Does Not Imply Reachability: Boundary Repair in Block-Sparse Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02680)
*Zhibo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现固定块稀疏因果注意力中序列局部性与注意力图可达性之间存在错配：相邻Token可能在每层被断开，导致边界伪影。作者通过结构依赖集形式化该问题，证明固定块掩码下目标表示仅依赖块内前缀，产生上界为1/K的准确率。提出相位条件覆盖函数预测稀疏模式失效场景，并给出边界桥接注意力作为零参数修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏因果注意力通常假设序列局部性（近处Token易访问，远处可丢弃），但本文发现固定块因果注意力中，相邻Token可能在每一层都被注意力图断开，形成边界伪影。这种"局部性-可达性"的错配未被先前工作充分认识，亟需理论化诊断和修复。

Method: (1) 通过结构依赖集形式化边界伪影：若每层使用相同固定块因果掩码且其余操作逐位置，目标表示仅依赖块前缀；(2) 推导K路边界复制分布的理论上界（top-1精度1/K，交叉熵下限log K）；(3) 提出相位条件覆盖函数，刻画可达性依赖源-目标距离及目标块内偏移；(4) 设计边界桥接注意力：保留固定块路径，在边界处用共享投影添加零额外参数的辅助因果边。

Result: 1024 Token实验中，性能提升集中在覆盖对齐的诊断任务上。在8K Token Qwen2.5-7B固定检查点探针实验中，观察到相同的覆盖不可比模式，提供了外部效度证据。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了块稀疏因果注意力中局部性-可达性错配的理论引导诊断框架、相位条件覆盖分析，以及一种最小化构造性修复方案（边界桥接注意力），揭示了滑动窗口注意力和边界修复不可互换的本质。

Abstract: Sparse causal attention is usually described by sequence locality: nearby tokens should remain easy to access, while distant tokens may be dropped to reduce cost. This paper studies a mismatch between sequence locality and attention-graph reachability. In fixed block causal attention, two adjacent tokens can be disconnected in the attention graph at every depth. We formalize this boundary artifact through structural dependency sets: if every attention layer uses the same fixed block causal mask and all remaining operations are positionwise, a target representation can depend only on tokens in its own block prefix. This yields an architecture-level boundary-copy separation for a constructed K-way boundary-copy distribution, with top-1 accuracy upper bound 1/K and expected cross-entropy lower bound log K. We then derive phase-conditioned coverage functions showing that reachability depends on both source-target distance and the target's offset within its block. These coverage laws predict when a sparse pattern should fail, when a repair can help, and why sliding-window attention and boundary repair are not interchangeable. Boundary Bridge Attention is treated as a constructive witness: it preserves the fixed block path and adds zero-additional-parameter auxiliary causal edges near block boundaries using shared projections. Controlled 1024-token experiments show that gains concentrate in coverage-aligned diagnostics. As secondary external-validity evidence, a fixed-checkpoint 8K-token Qwen2.5-7B probe shows the same coverage-incomparability pattern. The contribution is a theory-guided diagnostic framework for locality-reachability mismatch in block-sparse causal attention, together with phase-conditioned coverage analysis and a minimal constructive repair.

</details>


### [114] [Filter, Then Reweight: Rethinking Optimization Granularity in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02684)
*Yuying Li,Leqi Zheng,Yongzi Yu,Wenrui Zhou,Xuchang Zhong,Xing Hu,Jing Jin,Huangjie Yuan,Tao Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FiRe-OPD方法，通过在轨迹和token两个层级联合进行过滤与软重加权，实现更细粒度的在线策略蒸馏优化，在强到弱、单教师和多教师场景下均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略蒸馏方法正从全局KL监督转向选择性训练范式，但如何在轨迹和token层面同时进行精细化优化仍待探索。

Method: FiRe-OPD首先过滤低质量rollout轨迹，然后对保留轨迹内的token进行软重加权，以软权重机制替代硬选择，减少信息损失并增强优化稳定性。

Result: 在AIME 2024上强到弱设置提升+6.25，在Miner上多教师设置提升+18.81，优于近期token级OPD方法。

Conclusion: 轨迹与token层级的联合过滤-重加权策略能有效提升在线策略蒸馏性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: On-Policy distillation (OPD) in large language models is shifting from full-trace KL supervision toward more selective training paradigms. Recent OPD methods increasingly focus on selecting which trajectories to learn from, which tokens are most informative, and which supervision signals are most reliable. Motivated by this trend, we rethink optimization granularity of OPD and propose \fireicon\ FiRe-OPD (Filter, then Reweight), which jointly adjusts supervision signals at both trajectory and token levels. In details, FiRe-OPD first filters trajectories to remove low-quality rollout samples, and then applies soft reweighting within the retained trajectories to emphasize informative tokens. Compared with hard token selection, FiRe-OPD leverages a soft-weighting mechanism to effectively mitigate information loss and enhance optimization stability, thereby achieving finer-grained OPD optimization. We validate the effectiveness of FiRe-OPD across strong-to-weak, single-teacher, and multi-teacher settings, and demonstrate its superiority over recent token-level OPD methods ( (e.g., +6.25 on AIME 2024 in strong-to-weak, +18.81 on Miner in multi-teacher). Our code is available at https://github.com/YuYingLi0/FiRe-OPD.

</details>


### [115] [$Ψ$-Bench: Evaluating Persona-Sensitive Influencing in Persuasive Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02754)
*Peixuan Han,Hongyi Du,Jiayu Liu,Yihang Sun,Yutong Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ψ-Bench基准，用于评估大语言模型在真实交互场景中通过对话影响用户的能力，发现即使最先进的模型在说服力上仍有较大提升空间，而用户画像信息能带来18.24%的平均性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将个性化智能体定位为被动响应用户偏好的角色，限制了其主动与用户交互、提供建议或引导的能力，因此需要系统评估LLMs在真实交互中的主动个性化能力。

Method: 设计了包含说服任务的三个真实世界交互场景，通过从对话历史中提取显式用户画像来赋予模拟客户端个性化特征，并评估了10个前沿LLM在Ψ-Bench上的表现。

Result: 大多数模型能生成连贯合理的论点，但即使最先进的模型在说服力上仍有较大提升空间；提供用户画像信息可带来18.24%的平均性能提升。

Conclusion: 对人物画像敏感的影响是评估和开发更具主动性的个性化LLM智能体的一个具有挑战性且实用的方向。

Abstract: Personalization is a crucial capability of modern language agents. However, current research primarily positions personalized agents as passive responders to user preferences, limiting their ability to interact with users and provide suggestions or guidance proactively. To systematically evaluate such proactive personalization in realistic interactions, we propose $Ψ$-Bench, a benchmark for assessing LLMs' ability to influence realistic users through conversation. We design three real-world interaction scenarios that involve persuasion in $Ψ$-Bench, and endow simulated clients with personal characteristics through explicit user profiles derived from dialogue histories. We evaluate 10 frontier LLMs on $Ψ$-Bench and find that while most models can produce coherent and reasonable arguments, even state-of-the-art models still leave considerable room for improvement in persuasion. We also find that providing access to client profiles yields an average performance gain of 18.24\%, highlighting the importance of user-specific information for effective persuasion. Overall, our work highlights persona-sensitive influencing as a challenging yet practical direction for evaluating and developing more proactive personalized LLM agents. Codes are available at: https://github.com/Hanpx20/Psi-Bench.

</details>


### [116] [Binary Road Surface Classification Using Machine Learning on Production Vehicle Signals During Cruising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02762)
*Vishal Hariharan,Salar Basiri,Kanwar Bharat Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用基于特征和端到端的数据驱动方法，利用车辆动态信号（如轮速、扭矩、加速度等）在巡航状态下进行路面抓地力分类（干/湿 vs. 雪/冰），解决了传统动力学方法在低滑移工况下失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于动力学的摩擦估计方法在车辆巡航（低滑移）时失效，因为现有传感器无法精确测量微滑移，需要利用机器学习挖掘车辆信号与路面条件之间的关联。

Method: 采用基于特征的框架和端到端数据驱动框架，通过滑动窗口批处理轮速、轮扭矩、纵向加速度、转向角和横摆角速度等信号，输入机器学习模块进行二分类（抓地/滑移状态）。

Result: 在公共道路数据上的验证结果表明，该方法在巡航场景下能正确识别路面状况，展现了数据驱动摩擦估计在轮胎与车辆动力学领域的潜力。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的摩擦状态估计器在巡航工况下具有可行性，可弥补传统动力学方法的不足，为车辆预警和干预控制系统提供更实时可靠的路面抓地力信息。

Abstract: Knowledge of real-time road slipperiness, or even better, a refined estimate of peak grip potential, is a critical input for vehicle warning and intervention control systems. Typically, friction is estimated through dynamics-based recursive estimators by calculating the slip slope; however, its efficacy is heavily constrained by the vehicle dynamic scenario. When the vehicle is cruising and there is little to no slip, these methods become ineffective due to the inability of present-day production-grade sensors, such as wheel speed sensors, and methods to either measure or accurately estimate micro slip, which is crucial for distinguishing different surfaces.
